   1++6CAC              ; ============================================================================
   2++6CAC              ; Project127 ATM Core — linked kernel image layout measurement
   3++6CAC              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++6CAC              ;
   5++6CAC              ; Measures the actual assembled Stage2 kernel span [p127_entry,p127_end).
   6++6CAC              ; It is not a fixed reservation: the linked end label determines the size.
   7++6CAC              ; The current loader contract places the image at 6000h and bounds it by A000h.
   8++6CAC              ; Both live host pages used by that CPU window (P5 and P2) must be RESERVED.
   9++6CAC              ; This module validates placement only; it does not relocate or map pages.
  10++6CAC              ; ============================================================================
  11++6CAC              P127_LAYOUT_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  12++6CAC              P127_LAYOUT_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  13++6CAC              P127_LAYOUT_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  14++6CAC              P127_LAYOUT_START         EQU 06000h
  15++6CAC              P127_LAYOUT_LIMIT         EQU 0A000h
  16++6CAC              P127_LAYOUT_MAX_BYTES     EQU P127_LAYOUT_LIMIT-P127_LAYOUT_START
  17++6CAC
  18++6CAC              p127_layout_selftest:
  19++6CAC                      ; The linked entry must match the BASIC loader's CODE address.
  20++6CAC 21 00 60             ld      hl,p127_entry
  21++6CAF 11 00 60             ld      de,P127_LAYOUT_START
  22++6CB2 B7                   or      a
  23++6CB3 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  24++6CB5 C2 F4 6C             jp      nz,p127_layout_reject
  25++6CB8
  26++6CB8                      ; Require a non-empty image and an end no higher than the UI window.
  27++6CB8 21 04 90             ld      hl,p127_end
  28++6CBB 11 00 60             ld      de,p127_entry
  29++6CBE B7                   or      a
  30++6CBF ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  31++6CC1 DA F4 6C             jp      c,p127_layout_reject
  32++6CC4 CA F4 6C             jp      z,p127_layout_reject
  33++6CC7 22 0E 6D             ld      (p127_kernel_size),hl
  34++6CCA 7C                   ld      a,h
  35++6CCB FE 41                cp      041h
  36++6CCD D2 F4 6C             jp      nc,p127_layout_reject
  37++6CD0
  38++6CD0 21 04 90             ld      hl,p127_end
  39++6CD3 11 00 A0             ld      de,P127_LAYOUT_LIMIT
  40++6CD6 B7                   or      a
  41++6CD7 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  42++6CD9 DA E2 6C             jp      c,p127_layout_end_ok
  43++6CDC CA E2 6C             jp      z,p127_layout_end_ok
  44++6CDF C3 F4 6C             jp      p127_layout_reject
  45++6CE2              p127_layout_end_ok:
  46++6CE2                      ; P5 backs the #6000..#7FFF portion; P2 backs #8000..#9FFF.
  47++6CE2 3A F1 73             ld      a,(p127_mem_page_state+5)
  48++6CE5 FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
  49++6CE7 C2 F4 6C             jp      nz,p127_layout_reject
  50++6CEA 3A EE 73             ld      a,(p127_mem_page_state+2)
  51++6CED FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
  52++6CEF C2 F4 6C             jp      nz,p127_layout_reject
  53++6CF2 B7                   or      a
  54++6CF3 C9                   ret
  55++6CF4
  56++6CF4              p127_layout_reject:
  57++6CF4 37                   scf
  58++6CF5 C9                   ret
  59++6CF6
  60++6CF6              ; Report the measured byte count in hexadecimal, high byte first.
  61++6CF6              p127_layout_report:
  62++6CF6 21 10 6D             ld      hl,p127_msg_kernel_size
  63++6CF9 CD C7 62             call    p127_puts
  64++6CFC 3A 0F 6D             ld      a,(p127_kernel_size+1)
  65++6CFF CD E3 62             call    p127_put_hex8
  66++6D02 3A 0E 6D             ld      a,(p127_kernel_size)
  67++6D05 CD E3 62             call    p127_put_hex8
  68++6D08 21 2C 6D             ld      hl,p127_msg_kernel_size_tail
  69++6D0B C3 C7 62             jp      p127_puts
  70++6D0E
  71++6D0E              p127_kernel_size:
  72++6D0E 00 00                dw      0
  73++6D10              p127_msg_kernel_size:

  74++6D14 37 5D 20 4B

  74++6D1C 4C 20 49 4D

   1++6D48              ; ============================================================================
   2++6D48              ; Project127 — resident-window contract / software preflight
   3++6D48              ; Module version: 0.4.0
   4++6D48              ; Defines the only first-stage remap candidate without touching hardware.
   5++6D48              ; W1/P5 owns resident execution+data; W2/P2 owns resident stack/control.
   6++6D48              ; W3 is the only candidate target and may be used only after UI quiescence.
   7++6D48              ; W0 is ROM. No OUT instruction is permitted here.
   8++6D48              ; ============================================================================
   9++6D48              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  10++6D48              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_MINOR EQU 4
  11++6D48              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  12++6D48
  13++6D48              ; The remap-critical resident module is deliberately placed first after the
  14++6D48              ; Stage2 boot body. W1/P5 is the fixed execution/data window; W2/P2 is the
  15++6D48              ; fixed stack window; W3 is the only target. This preflight checks the linked
  16++6D48              ; addresses and current live stack at runtime. It performs no page OUT.
  17++6D48              p127_resident_start:
  18++6D48
  19++6D48              P127_RESIDENT_CODE_WINDOW  EQU 1
  20++6D48              P127_RESIDENT_CODE_PAGE    EQU 5
  21++6D48              P127_RESIDENT_STACK_WINDOW EQU 2
  22++6D48              P127_RESIDENT_STACK_PAGE   EQU 2
  23++6D48              P127_RESIDENT_TARGET       EQU 3
  24++6D48              P127_RESIDENT_UI_QUIESCED  EQU 1
  25++6D48
  26++6D48              ; IN: A=target window, B=resident code window, C=resident stack window,
  27++6D48              ;     D=flags (bit 0 set means UI is quiesced).
  28++6D48              ; OUT: carry=0 if the fixed resident contract is satisfied; carry=1 otherwise.
  29++6D48              ; This is a software contract check, not a physical mapping operation.
  30++6D48              p127_resident_validate:
  31++6D48 32 D2 6D             ld      (p127_resident_arg_target),a
  32++6D4B 78                   ld      a,b
  33++6D4C 32 D3 6D             ld      (p127_resident_arg_code),a
  34++6D4F 79                   ld      a,c
  35++6D50 32 D4 6D             ld      (p127_resident_arg_stack),a
  36++6D53 7A                   ld      a,d
  37++6D54 32 D5 6D             ld      (p127_resident_arg_flags),a
  38++6D57
  39++6D57 3A D2 6D             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_target)
  40++6D5A FE 03                cp      P127_RESIDENT_TARGET
  41++6D5C C2 86 6D             jp      nz,p127_resident_reject
  42++6D5F 3A D3 6D             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_code)
  43++6D62 FE 01                cp      P127_RESIDENT_CODE_WINDOW
  44++6D64 C2 86 6D             jp      nz,p127_resident_reject
  45++6D67 3A D4 6D             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_stack)
  46++6D6A FE 02                cp      P127_RESIDENT_STACK_WINDOW
  47++6D6C C2 86 6D             jp      nz,p127_resident_reject
  48++6D6F 3A D5 6D             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_flags)
  49++6D72 E6 01                and     P127_RESIDENT_UI_QUIESCED
  50++6D74 CA 86 6D             jp      z,p127_resident_reject
  51++6D77                      ; Explicitly assert target is distinct from both resident windows.
  52++6D77 3A D2 6D             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_target)
  53++6D7A FE 01                cp      P127_RESIDENT_CODE_WINDOW
  54++6D7C CA 86 6D             jp      z,p127_resident_reject
  55++6D7F FE 02                cp      P127_RESIDENT_STACK_WINDOW
  56++6D81 CA 86 6D             jp      z,p127_resident_reject
  57++6D84 B7                   or      a
  58++6D85 C9                   ret
  59++6D86
  60++6D86              p127_resident_reject:
  61++6D86 37                   scf
  62++6D87 C9                   ret
  63++6D88
  64++6D88              ; Synthetic software-only tests: accepted W3 with UI quiesced; reject W1,
  65++6D88              ; reject W2, reject active UI, and reject a displaced resident code window.
  66++6D88              p127_resident_selftest:
  67++6D88 3E 03                ld      a,3
  68++6D8A 06 01                ld      b,1
  69++6D8C 0E 02                ld      c,2
  70++6D8E 16 01                ld      d,1
  71++6D90 CD 48 6D             call    p127_resident_validate
  72++6D93 DA D0 6D             jp      c,p127_resident_test_fail
  73++6D96

  80++6DA4

 248++6EBA              ; Save/restore the complete software W3 ownership tuple.
 249++6EBA              ; Snapshot and rollback are RAM-only; they never touch page registers.

 268++6EE1 C9                   ret

 270++6EE2              ; Recovery tests: reject a competing acquire, preserve a busy UI when
 271++6EE2              ; quiesce is refused, roll back a partial HANDOFF acquire, reject a

 273++6EE2              p127_w3_recovery_selftest:

 349++6F79              ; Stage 2.10 — real-address resident trampoline preflight (software only).
 350++6F79              ; Requires DI on entry; DI is asserted again and interrupts remain disabled.
 351++6F79              ; Z80 does not provide a general instruction to read/restore prior IFF1, so
 352++6F79              ; this entry contract intentionally does not attempt an EI/IFF round trip.
 353++6F79              ; W1 is #4000-#7FFF; W2 is #8000-#BFFF. The linked resident module must fit
 354++6F79              ; wholly in W1, all resident state is inside that module, and the live stack
 355++6F79              ; must be in W2. No page register is written.

   1++6FC8              ; ============================================================================
   2++6FC8              ; Project127 — interrupt safety contract / preflight
   3++6FC8              ; Module version: 0.5.0
   4++6FC8              ; This is deliberately a DI-only bootstrap contract, not an IRQ dispatcher.
   5++6FC8              ; No EI, IM instruction, vector write, or hardware page OUT is permitted.
   6++6FC8              ; ============================================================================
   7++6FC8              P127_IRQ_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
   8++6FC8              P127_IRQ_VERSION_MINOR EQU 5
   9++6FC8              P127_IRQ_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  10++6FC8              P127_IRQ_MASKED EQU 1
  11++6FC8              P127_IRQ_VECTOR_NONE EQU 0
  12++6FC8              P127_IRQ_MODE_UNSET EQU 0
  13++6FC8
  14++6FC8              ; Boot owns the CPU in a DI-protected phase. A stock Z80 cannot read IM0/1/2
  15++6FC8              ; directly; LD A,I exposes IFF2 in P/V but is not a general IFF restore path.
  16++6FC8              ; Therefore this module never guesses prior interrupt state and never EI/IM.
  17++6FC8              ; Until a real vector table/handler is installed, vector ownership is NONE.
  18++6FC8              ; Interrupts may not be enabled until all three prerequisites are explicitly
  19++6FC8              ; established by later stages: writable/owned vector table, verified table
  20++6FC8              ; mapping, and installed resident handler. No current boot code sets these.
  21++6FC8              P127_IRQ_READY_VECTOR EQU 1
  22++6FC8              P127_IRQ_READY_MAP EQU 2
  23++6FC8              P127_IRQ_READY_HANDLER EQU 4
  24++6FC8              P127_IRQ_READY_ALL EQU 7
  25++6FC8
  26++6FC8              p127_irq_init:
  27++6FC8 F3                   di
  28++6FC9 3E 01                ld      a,P127_IRQ_MASKED
  29++6FCB 32 AA 72             ld      (p127_irq_mask_state),a
  30++6FCE AF                   xor     a
  31++6FCF 32 AB 72             ld      (p127_irq_vector_owner),a
  32++6FD2 32 AC 72             ld      (p127_irq_mode_policy),a
  33++6FD5 32 AD 72             ld      (p127_irq_ready_flags),a
  34++6FD8 B7                   or      a
  35++6FD9 C9                   ret
  36++6FDA
  37++6FDA              ; OUT: carry clear only while the documented DI-only/no-vector contract holds.
  38++6FDA              p127_irq_validate:

   1++72AE              ; ============================================================================
   2++72AE              ; Project127 ATM Core — physical page-state manager
   3++72AE              ; Module version: 0.4.3
   4++72AE              ; ABI: reserve A=first page, B=count; alloc -> A=page, C=exhausted;
   5++72AE              ;      free A=page, C=invalid/not allocated.
   6++72AE              ; Model: 64 physical 16 KiB page IDs = exactly 1 MiB.
   7++72AE              ; ATM Turbo 2+ page IDs are physical 16 KiB RAM pages. Bootstrap reservations
   8++72AE              ; reflect the active ZX-128 map: page 5 (window 1), page 2 (window 2), and
   9++72AE              ; page 0 (window 3). Page 5 is already covered by the video-page union.
  10++72AE              ; ============================================================================
  11++72AE              P127_MEM_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  12++72AE              P127_MEM_VERSION_MINOR EQU 4
  13++72AE              P127_MEM_VERSION_PATCH EQU 3

  16++72AE              P127_MEM_FREE          EQU 0
  17++72AE              P127_MEM_RESERVED      EQU 1

   1++742C              ; ============================================================================
   2++742C              ; Project127 ATM Core — UINT32 physical range validator
   3++742C              ; Module version: 0.1.2
   4++742C              ; ABI:
   5++742C              ;   p127_range_start  = 4-byte little-endian physical start address
   6++742C              ;   p127_range_length = 4-byte little-endian byte length
   7++742C              ;   call p127_range_validate; carry=1 if invalid, clear if valid; clobbers AF, B, DE, HL
   8++742C              ; Semantics: half-open [start, start+length), bounded by 1 MiB.
   9++742C              ; Zero-length ranges are valid when start <= 1 MiB.
  10++742C              ; This is arithmetic/accounting only; it does not map or access RAM.
  11++742C              ; ============================================================================
  12++742C              P127_RANGE_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  13++742C              P127_RANGE_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  14++742C              P127_RANGE_VERSION_PATCH EQU 2
  15++742C              P127_RANGE_LIMIT         EQU 0100000h
  16++742C
  17++742C              p127_range_validate:
  18++742C                      ; Add the unsigned 32-bit length to the unsigned start.
  19++742C 21 3A 75             ld      hl,p127_range_start
  20++742F 11 3E 75             ld      de,p127_range_length
  21++7432 1A                   ld      a,(de)
  22++7433 47                   ld      b,a
  23++7434 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
  24++7435 80                   add     a,b
  25++7436 32 42 75             ld      (p127_range_end),a
  26++7439 23                   inc     hl
  27++743A 13                   inc     de
  28++743B 1A                   ld      a,(de)
  29++743C 47                   ld      b,a
  30++743D 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
  31++743E 88                   adc     a,b
  32++743F 32 43 75             ld      (p127_range_end+1),a
  33++7442 23                   inc     hl
  34++7443 13                   inc     de
  35++7444 1A                   ld      a,(de)
  36++7445 47                   ld      b,a
  37++7446 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
  38++7447 88                   adc     a,b
  39++7448 32 44 75             ld      (p127_range_end+2),a
  40++744B 23                   inc     hl
  41++744C 13                   inc     de
  42++744D 1A                   ld      a,(de)
  43++744E 47                   ld      b,a
  44++744F 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
  45++7450 88                   adc     a,b
  46++7451 32 45 75             ld      (p127_range_end+3),a
  47++7454 D8                   ret     c                       ; unsigned 32-bit addition overflow
  48++7455
  49++7455                      ; End-exclusive address must be <= 0x00100000.
  50++7455 3A 45 75             ld      a,(p127_range_end+3)
  51++7458 B7                   or      a
  52++7459 C2 77 74             jp      nz,p127_range_invalid
  53++745C 3A 44 75             ld      a,(p127_range_end+2)
  54++745F FE 10                cp      010h
  55++7461 DA 75 74             jp      c,p127_range_valid
  56++7464 C2 77 74             jp      nz,p127_range_invalid
  57++7467 3A 43 75             ld      a,(p127_range_end+1)
  58++746A B7                   or      a
  59++746B C2 77 74             jp      nz,p127_range_invalid
  60++746E 3A 42 75             ld      a,(p127_range_end)
  61++7471 B7                   or      a
  62++7472 C2 77 74             jp      nz,p127_range_invalid
  63++7475              p127_range_valid:
  64++7475 B7                   or      a
  65++7476 C9                   ret
  66++7477              p127_range_invalid:
  67++7477 37                   scf
  68++7478 C9                   ret
  69++7479
  70++7479              ; Regression tests exercise the 1 MiB boundary, zero length and uint32 wrap.
  71++7479              ; OUT: carry=1 on any failed assertion.
  72++7479              p127_range_selftest:
  73++7479                      ; [0, 0) is valid.
  74++7479 AF                   xor     a
  75++747A 21 3A 75             ld      hl,p127_range_start
  76++747D 77                   ld      (hl),a

  87++748A 23                   inc     hl

  92++7491 DA 38 75             jp      c,p127_range_test_fail

   1++7546              ; ============================================================================
   2++7546              ; Project127 ATM Core — top-down physical program-page arena
   3++7546              ; Module version: 0.1.2
   4++7546              ;
   5++7546              ; Arena discovers the highest contiguous FREE physical run from the page-state
   6++7546              ; map. The current reservation map yields P8..P63; P6 remains isolated below
   7++7546              ; reserved P7. Physical pages are 16 KiB and P63 ends at 0x100000.
   8++7546              ;
   9++7546              ; API:
  10++7546              ;   p127_arena_init: discovers and validates the highest contiguous FREE run.
  11++7546              ;   p127_arena_alloc: IN B=page count; OUT A=first page, carry=1 on failure.
  12++7546              ;                    Allocates downward from the current high boundary.
  13++7546              ;   p127_arena_free: IN A=first page, B=count; only most-recent block accepted.
  14++7546              ;                   Frees it and contracts the high boundary upward.
  15++7546              ;
  16++7546              ; This is page-granular accounting only. It does not map pages, access RAM,
  17++7546              ; provide byte-granular allocation, or promise a seamless legacy 16-bit ABI.
  18++7546              ; Free is intentionally LIFO until per-process ownership/coalescing exists.
  19++7546              ; ============================================================================
  20++7546              P127_ARENA_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  21++7546              P127_ARENA_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  22++7546              P127_ARENA_VERSION_PATCH EQU 2
  23++7546              P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE      EQU 64
  24++7546              P127_ARENA_PAGE_SIZE      EQU 04000h
  25++7546
  26++7546              ; Discover the highest contiguous FREE run from the actual page-state map.
  27++7546              ; The first RESERVED/ALLOCATED page below that run is the lower guard.
  28++7546              ; An all-FREE map is rejected: required system reservations are missing.
  29++7546              p127_arena_init:
  30++7546 21 2B 74             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state+P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE-1
  31++7549 0E 3F                ld      c,P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE-1

  37++7552 2B                   dec     hl
  38++7553 0D                   dec     c
  39++7554 10 F7                djnz    .p127_arena_init_scan
  40++7556 C3 0C 76             jp      p127_arena_reject
  41++7559              .p127_arena_init_guard_found:

  46++7560 32 9D 76             ld      (p127_arena_low_page),a
  47++7563 3E 40                ld      a,P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE

  49++7566 CA 0C 76             jp      z,p127_arena_reject
  50++7569 32 9E 76             ld      (p127_arena_page_count),a

  52++756E 32 9F 76             ld      (p127_arena_next_page),a
  53++7571 B7                   or      a
  54++7572 C9                   ret
  55++7573

   1++76A4              ; ============================================================================
   2++76A4              ; Project127 ATM Core — managed logical address-space page table
   3++76A4              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++76A4              ;
   5++76A4              ; 4 MiB logical space: 256 logical pages x 16 KiB. A logical page maps to one
   6++76A4              ; allocator-owned physical ATM page (0..63). Translation metadata only:
   7++76A4              ; no CPU-window mapping, physical byte access, IN/OUT or DMA.
   8++76A4              ;
   9++76A4              ; API:
  10++76A4              ;   p127_as_init: clear all slots to FFh (unmapped).
  11++76A4              ;   p127_as_bind: A=logical page 0..255, B=allocated physical page 0..63.
  12++76A4              ;   p127_as_unbind: A=logical page; removes metadata, does not free RAM.
  13++76A4              ;   p127_as_translate: p127_as_address (uint32 LE) -> A=physical page,
  14++76A4              ;                      HL=14-bit page offset; carry=1 if invalid/unmapped.
  15++76A4              ; Caller must unbind before releasing a physical page.
  16++76A4              ; ============================================================================
  17++76A4              P127_AS_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  18++76A4              P127_AS_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  19++76A4              P127_AS_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  20++76A4              P127_AS_LOGICAL_PAGE_COUNT EQU 256
  21++76A4              P127_AS_PAGE_SIZE EQU 04000h
  22++76A4              P127_AS_UNMAPPED EQU 0FFh
  23++76A4

  26++76A7 36 FF                ld      (hl),P127_AS_UNMAPPED
  27++76A9 11 46 78             ld      de,p127_as_page_table+1

   1++7B25              ; ============================================================================
   2++7B25              ; Project127 — managed logical-span copy
   3++7B25              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++7B25              ;
   5++7B25              ; Copies between non-overlapping mapped logical spans using the guarded W3
   6++7B25              ; lease and a resident 64-byte bounce buffer. W1/P5 holds this code/buffer;
   7++7B25              ; W2/P2 holds the live stack. No UI call occurs while W3 is leased.
   8++7B25              ;
   9++7B25              ; API inputs:
  10++7B25              ;   p127_as_copy_source      uint32 little-endian
  11++7B25              ;   p127_as_copy_destination uint32 little-endian
  12++7B25              ;   p127_as_copy_length      uint32 little-endian
  13++7B25              ; Outputs:
  14++7B25              ;   carry clear = success; carry set = failure
  15++7B25              ;   p127_as_copy_completed   exact bytes written before return
  16++7B25              ;   p127_as_copy_error       0 success, 1 span, 2 overlap, 3 lease,
  17++7B25              ;                            4 source map, 5 destination map, 6 lease restore
  18++7B25              ; Overlap is rejected; this is not memmove. Unexpected runtime failures after
  19++7B25              ; writes begin may leave a destination prefix modified.
  20++7B25              ; ============================================================================

  22++7B25              P127_AS_COPY_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1

 178++81D3              ; Safe-remap preparation gate (software-only).
 179++81D3              ; IN: A=target window 0..3, B=new physical page 0..63,
 180++81D3              ;     C=bitmask of protected windows (bit 0=W0 ... bit 3=W3).
 181++81D3              ; OUT: carry=1 if invalid/protected; carry=0 if a pending request is staged.
 182++81D3              ; This routine NEVER writes a page port and NEVER changes the active map.
 183++81D3              ; Caller must include execution, stack, UI, interrupt and host/staging windows
 184++81D3              ; in C. The bootstrap currently protects all four windows (#0F).
 185++81D3              p127_map_handoff_prepare:

   1++83C9              ; ============================================================================
   2++83C9              ; Project127 — resident stack handoff probe
   3++83C9              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++83C9              ; Runs from the resident Stage2 image in W1/P5. Scratch stack is a bounded
   5++83C9              ; test area in the still-mapped W2/P2. No page port is written.
   6++83C9              ; ============================================================================
   7++83C9              P127_HANDOFF_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
   8++83C9              P127_HANDOFF_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
   9++83C9              P127_HANDOFF_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  10++83C9
  11++83C9              ; Current bootstrap stack is at #BFFF in W2/P2. Keep at least 128 bytes
  12++83C9              ; between its low-water boundary and the scratch stack.
  13++83C9              P127_HANDOFF_MIN_OLD_SP    EQU 0B900h
  14++83C9              P127_HANDOFF_STACK_BOTTOM  EQU 0B800h
  15++83C9              P127_HANDOFF_STACK_TOP     EQU 0B880h
  16++83C9              P127_HANDOFF_GUARD_LO      EQU 0B7FEh
  17++83C9              P127_HANDOFF_GUARD_HI      EQU 0B7FFh
  18++83C9
  19++83C9              ; IN: none.
  20++83C9              ; OUT: carry=1 on failure; carry=0 on success.
  21++83C9              ; Preserves BC, DE, HL, IX and IY. AF/flags are return status.
  22++83C9              ; Requires interrupts disabled. DI is asserted defensively and this routine
  23++83C9              ; returns with interrupts disabled. IM and interrupt vectors are untouched.
  24++83C9              ; The active stack must be at/above #B900 after this routine's save frame.
  25++83C9              ; This is a stack-integrity probe only: W2 remains mapped throughout.
  26++83C9              p127_handoff_stack_probe:
  27++83C9 F3                   di
  28++83CA F5                   push    af
  29++83CB C5                   push    bc
  30++83CC D5                   push    de
  31++83CD E5                   push    hl
  32++83CE DD E5                push    ix
  33++83D0 FD E5                push    iy
  34++83D2 ED 73 61 84          ld      (p127_handoff_saved_sp),sp
  35++83D6
  36++83D6                      ; Refuse if the live stack has crossed its declared low-water limit.
  37++83D6 21 00 00             ld      hl,0
  38++83D9 39                   add     hl,sp
  39++83DA 11 00 B9             ld      de,P127_HANDOFF_MIN_OLD_SP
  40++83DD B7                   or      a
  41++83DE ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  42++83E0 DA 31 84             jp      c,p127_handoff_stack_refuse
  43++83E3
  44++83E3                      ; Canary bytes bracket the unused gap below the scratch stack.
  45++83E3 3E A5                ld      a,0A5h
  46++83E5 32 FE B7             ld      (P127_HANDOFF_GUARD_LO),a
  47++83E8 3E 5A                ld      a,05Ah
  48++83EA 32 FF B7             ld      (P127_HANDOFF_GUARD_HI),a
  49++83ED
  50++83ED                      ; Switch only the software stack pointer. W2/P2 stays mapped.
  51++83ED 31 80 B8             ld      sp,P127_HANDOFF_STACK_TOP
  52++83F0 21 57 13             ld      hl,01357h
  53++83F3 E5                   push    hl

   1++8464              ; ============================================================================
   2++8464              ; Project127 TR-DOS directory reader
   3++8464              ; Module version: 0.1.2
   4++8464              ; Reads track 0 sectors 1..8 via the documented TR-DOS ROM entry #3D13.
   5++8464              ; Directory snapshot: 128 * 16 bytes at #C800 (reserved host/staging page P0).
   6++8464              ; Read-only: no file-data reads, writes, DMA, IM/EI or raw paging OUT.
   7++8464              ; ============================================================================

   9++8464              P127_TRDOS_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  10++8464              P127_TRDOS_VERSION_PATCH EQU 2

  12++8464              ; Separate from the live caller stack at #BFFF and handoff probe stack #B800.
  13++8464              ; The 240-byte usable interval #B700..#B7EF is below the handoff canary.
  14++8464              P127_TRDOS_SCRATCH_STACK_TOP EQU 0B7F0h
  15++8464              P127_TRDOS_DIRECTORY_BYTES EQU 0800h
  16++8464              P127_TRDOS_ENTRY_COUNT EQU 128
  17++8464              P127_TRDOS_ENTRY_SIZE EQU 16
  18++8464

  20++8464              ; Output is mirrored by p127_puts/p127_put_char to the visible console and #EF.
  21++8464              ; Called immediately after the Project126-compatible legacy mapping
  22++8464              ; setup, before p127_ui_init changes the ROM/video/shadow-port state.

# file opened: p127_speaker.asm
   1++89B1              ; ============================================================================
   2++89B1              ; Project127 ATM Turbo 2+ beeper output driver
   3++89B1              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++89B1              ;
   5++89B1              ; Hardware: Spectrum/ATM #FE, D4 = speaker, D3 = tape output,
   6++89B1              ; D0..D2 = border colour. OUT (#FE),A also presents A3 from A's bit 3
   7++89B1              ; on ATM, so the caller-supplied D3 state is preserved.
   8++89B1              ;
   9++89B1              ; This is a low-level output latch only. It does not invent a timer,
  10++89B1              ; enable interrupts, block the main loop, or emulate an IBM PC speaker.
  11++89B1              ; The Z80 host owns this physical port. NeoGS must request sound through
  12++89B1              ; the host transport/DMA and must never issue physical port I/O.
  13++89B1              ;
  14++89B1              ; API:
  15++89B1              ;   p127_speaker_init  IN A = initial #FE low nibble (border + tape)
  16++89B1              ;   p127_speaker_set   IN A bit 0 = speaker level (0/1)
  17++89B1              ;   p127_speaker_toggle
  18++89B1              ;   p127_speaker_stop
  19++89B1              ;   p127_speaker_get   OUT A = shadow latch
  20++89B1              ;
  21++89B1              ; Caller must initialize with the actual current border/tape state before
  22++89B1              ; using set/toggle/stop. No safe hardware readback of the #FE output latch
  23++89B1              ; is assumed.
  24++89B1              ; ============================================================================
  25++89B1              P127_SPEAKER_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  26++89B1              P127_SPEAKER_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  27++89B1              P127_SPEAKER_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  28++89B1
  29++89B1              P127_SPEAKER_PORT EQU 0FEh
  30++89B1              P127_SPEAKER_MASK EQU 010h
  31++89B1              P127_SPEAKER_LOW_MASK EQU 00Fh
  32++89B1
  33++89B1              p127_speaker_init:
  34++89B1 E6 0F                and     P127_SPEAKER_LOW_MASK
  35++89B3 32 0F 8A             ld      (p127_speaker_shadow),a
  36++89B6 C9                   ret
  37++89B7
  38++89B7              ; Set D4 while preserving border D0..D2 and tape D3.
  39++89B7              ; OUT (#FE),A intentionally uses A3 as part of the ATM border/BRIGHT
  40++89B7              ; address decode; preserving D3 also preserves that address-line state.
  41++89B7              p127_speaker_set:
  42++89B7 C5                   push    bc
  43++89B8 47                   ld      b,a
  44++89B9 3A 0F 8A             ld      a,(p127_speaker_shadow)
  45++89BC E6 0F                and     P127_SPEAKER_LOW_MASK
  46++89BE 4F                   ld      c,a
  47++89BF 78                   ld      a,b
  48++89C0 E6 01                and     1
  49++89C2 28 05                jr      z,p127_speaker_set_low
  50++89C4 79                   ld      a,c
  51++89C5 F6 10                or      P127_SPEAKER_MASK
  52++89C7 18 01                jr      p127_speaker_write
  53++89C9              p127_speaker_set_low:
  54++89C9 79                   ld      a,c
  55++89CA              p127_speaker_write:
  56++89CA 32 0F 8A             ld      (p127_speaker_shadow),a
  57++89CD D3 FE                out     (P127_SPEAKER_PORT),a
  58++89CF C1                   pop     bc
  59++89D0 C9                   ret
  60++89D1
  61++89D1              ; Invert only D4. Border/tape state remains unchanged.
  62++89D1              p127_speaker_toggle:
  63++89D1 3A 0F 8A             ld      a,(p127_speaker_shadow)
  64++89D4 EE 10                xor     P127_SPEAKER_MASK
  65++89D6 32 0F 8A             ld      (p127_speaker_shadow),a
  66++89D9 D3 FE                out     (P127_SPEAKER_PORT),a
  67++89DB C9                   ret
  68++89DC
  69++89DC              ; Force speaker low without changing D0..D3.
  70++89DC              p127_speaker_stop:
  71++89DC 3A 0F 8A             ld      a,(p127_speaker_shadow)
  72++89DF E6 EF                and     0EFh
  73++89E1 32 0F 8A             ld      (p127_speaker_shadow),a
  74++89E4 D3 FE                out     (P127_SPEAKER_PORT),a
  75++89E6 C9                   ret
  76++89E7
  77++89E7              p127_speaker_get:
  78++89E7 3A 0F 8A             ld      a,(p127_speaker_shadow)
  79++89EA C9                   ret
  80++89EB
  81++89EB
  82++89EB              ; Audible hardware smoke test. Approximately 0.57 s at 7 MHz or 1.14 s at
  83++89EB              ; 3.5 MHz; pitch therefore depends on the active ATM CPU clock. This is
  84++89EB              ; intentionally a one-shot diagnostic, not the asynchronous sound service.
  85++89EB              ; A=low #FE nibble to preserve (caller must supply the live border/tape state).
  86++89EB              p127_speaker_test_tone:
  87++89EB F5                   push    af
  88++89EC C5                   push    bc
  89++89ED D5                   push    de
  90++89EE E5                   push    hl
  91++89EF E6 0F                and     P127_SPEAKER_LOW_MASK
  92++89F1 CD B1 89             call    p127_speaker_init
  93++89F4 11 58 02             ld      de,0258h
  94++89F7              p127_speaker_test_loop:
  95++89F7 CD D1 89             call    p127_speaker_toggle
  96++89FA 06 FF                ld      b,0FFh
  97++89FC              p127_speaker_test_delay1:
  98++89FC 10 FE                djnz    p127_speaker_test_delay1
  99++89FE 06 FF                ld      b,0FFh
 100++8A00              p127_speaker_test_delay2:
 101++8A00 10 FE                djnz    p127_speaker_test_delay2
 102++8A02 1B                   dec     de
 103++8A03 7A                   ld      a,d
 104++8A04 B3                   or      e
 105++8A05 20 F0                jr      nz,p127_speaker_test_loop
 106++8A07 CD DC 89             call    p127_speaker_stop
 107++8A0A E1                   pop     hl
 108++8A0B D1                   pop     de
 109++8A0C C1                   pop     bc
 110++8A0D F1                   pop     af
 111++8A0E C9                   ret
 112++8A0F
 113++8A0F 00           p127_speaker_shadow: db 0

   1++8A10              ; ============================================================================
   2++8A10              ; Project127 Nemo IDE HDD read-only transport
   3++8A10              ; Module version: 0.1.2
   4++8A10              ; Port map and LBA28 protocol adapted from Project127/113/src/nemo_ide.asm.
   5++8A10              ; Only single-sector ATA READ SECTORS (20h) is exposed in this stage.
   6++8A10              ; No ATA write command, DMA, IRQ, IM/EI or page-port access is used.

  11++8A10

  13++8A10              P127_HDD_PORT_DATA_HI   EQU 00011h
  14++8A10              P127_HDD_PORT_COUNT     EQU 00050h

   1++8CE6              ; ============================================================================
   2++8CE6              ; Project127 FAT16 root-directory reader
   3++8CE6              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++8CE6              ; Derived from Project127/113 FAT16 BPB/root-directory flow.
   5++8CE6              ; Uses the existing NEMO-DIVIDE LBA28 single-sector transport in p127_hdd.asm.
   6++8CE6              ; Read-only: no writes, DMA, IRQ, IM/EI or paging OUT.
   7++8CE6              ; Output: each root entry is mirrored to the ATM console and debug port #EF.
   8++8CE6              ; ============================================================================
   9++8CE6              P127_HDD_FAT16_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  10++8CE6              P127_HDD_FAT16_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  11++8CE6              P127_HDD_FAT16_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  12++8CE6
  13++8CE6              ; Parse the FAT16 BPB at LBA 63, calculate the absolute root-directory LBA,
  14++8CE6              ; read every root-directory sector and print each valid 8.3 entry.
  15++8CE6              ; IN: P127_HDD_SECTOR_BUFFER contains the partition boot sector from probe.
  16++8CE6              ; OUT: carry=1 on invalid BPB or sector-read failure.
  17++8CE6              p127_hdd_list_root:
  18++8CE6 F5                   push    af
  19++8CE7 C5                   push    bc
  20++8CE8 D5                   push    de
  21++8CE9 E5                   push    hl
  22++8CEA DD E5                push    ix
  23++8CEC FD E5                push    iy
  24++8CEE
  25++8CEE AF                   xor     a
  26++8CEF 32 E2 8F             ld      (p127_hdd_fat_error),a
  27++8CF2 32 F0 8F             ld      (p127_hdd_fat_file_count),a
  28++8CF5 32 F1 8F             ld      (p127_hdd_fat_file_count+1),a
  29++8CF8 21 2E 8F             ld      hl,p127_hdd_fat_msg_begin
  30++8CFB CD C7 62             call    p127_puts
  31++8CFE
  32++8CFE                      ; The transport already validated bytes/sector=512 and 55AA.
  33++8CFE                      ; Validate the FAT16 fields needed to locate the fixed root directory.
  34++8CFE 3A 0D C8             ld      a,(P127_HDD_SECTOR_BUFFER+13)
  35++8D01 B7                   or      a
  36++8D02 CA 28 8E             jp      z,p127_hdd_fat_bad_bpb
  37++8D05 3A 10 C8             ld      a,(P127_HDD_SECTOR_BUFFER+16)
  38++8D08 B7                   or      a
  39++8D09 CA 28 8E             jp      z,p127_hdd_fat_bad_bpb
  40++8D0C 2A 11 C8             ld      hl,(P127_HDD_SECTOR_BUFFER+17)
  41++8D0F 7C                   ld      a,h
  42++8D10 B5                   or      l
  43++8D11 CA 28 8E             jp      z,p127_hdd_fat_bad_bpb
  44++8D14
  45++8D14                      ; Bound root entries to 4096 (256 sectors) to reject corrupt BPBs.
  46++8D14 7C                   ld      a,h
  47++8D15 FE 10                cp      10h
  48++8D17 38 08                jr      c,p127_hdd_fat_root_size_ok
  49++8D19 C2 28 8E             jp      nz,p127_hdd_fat_bad_bpb
  50++8D1C 7D                   ld      a,l

# file opened: p127_console.asm
   1+ A000              ; ============================================================================
   2+ A000              ; Project127 ATM-native debug console, ported from Project126
   3+ A000              ; Source: Project126/atm_host/p126_screen_console.asm
   4+ A000              ; Module version: 0.1.4
   5+ A000              ; ATM Turbo 2+ RG6 80x25; upper 12 rows status, lower 13 rows scrolling log.
   6+ A000              ; Every displayed debug character and static UI label is mirrored to #EF.
   7+ A000              ; Resident placement: #A000, in the Project126 host display window.
   8+ A000              ; ============================================================================
   9+ A000              P126_ATM_VIDEO_MODE_RG6_TEXT80X25 EQU 6
  10+ A000              BIOS_DEBUG_COLUMNS EQU 80
  11+ A000              BIOS_DEBUG_ROWS EQU 13
  12+ A000              BIOS_DEBUG_TOP EQU 12
  13+ A000              P126_VIDEO_PAGE7 EQU #78
  14+ A000              P126_VIDEO_PAGE3 EQU #7C
  15+ A000              P126_HOST_PAGE5 EQU #7A
  16+ A000              P126_HOST_PAGE0 EQU #7F
  17+ A000              P126_ATM_TEXT_WIDTH EQU 80
  18+ A000              P126_ATM_TEXT_HEIGHT EQU 25

  20+ A000              P127_UI_DEBUG_PORT EQU #00EF

  23+ A001 00           p126_atm_video_enabled: db 0
  24+ A002 00           Console_X: db 0
  25+ A003 0C           Console_Y: db BIOS_DEBUG_TOP
  26+ A004 00           bios_dbg_row: db 0
  27+ A005 00           bios_dbg_col: db 0
  28+ A006 00           bios_dbg_char: db 0
  29+ A007 00           bios_dbg_write_row: db 0
  30+ A008 00           bios_dbg_write_col: db 0

  38+ A011              p126_atm_video_init:
  38+ A011
  39+ A011 F5                   push    af
  40+ A012 C5                   push    bc
  41+ A013 D5                   push    de
  42+ A014 E5                   push    hl
  43+ A015
  44+ A015                      ; Reset the ATM paging/control latch BEFORE enabling permanent
  45+ A015                      ; DOSEN. On this board #EFF7 is part of the ATM paging control path;
  46+ A015                      ; resetting it after the #0177 shadow-port setup can undo the state
  47+ A015                      ; needed by the TR-DOS overlay.
  48+ A015 AF                   xor     a
  49+ A016 01 F7 EF             ld      bc, #EFF7
  50+ A019 ED 79                out     (c), a
  51+ A01B
  52+ A01B                      ; ROM2=1 (D4) is required for TR-DOS overlay; D3 selects alternate
  53+ A01B                      ; video pages 7/3. This is #18, matching the host display setup.
  54+ A01B 3E 18                ld      a, #18
  55+ A01D 01 FD 7F             ld      bc, #7FFD
  56+ A020 ED 79                out     (c), a
  57+ A022 32 3B 5C             ld      (#5C3B), a
  58+ A025
  59+ A025                      ; Open shadow ports and hold DOSEN permanently (A9=0) using the
  60+ A025                      ; documented TR-DOS ROM trampoline. Do this AFTER the paging reset.
  61+ A025 01 35 A0             ld      bc, p126_atm_ports_open_done
  62+ A028 C5                   push    bc
  63+ A029 01 53 2A             ld      bc, #2A53
  64+ A02C C5                   push    bc
  65+ A02D 01 77 01             ld      bc, #0177
  66+ A030 3E C3                ld      a, #C3
  67+ A032 C3 2F 3D             jp      #3D2F
  68+ A035
  69+ A035              p126_atm_ports_open_done:
  70+ A035                      ; Window 0 must use ROM with D7=1 so its ROM page selection is
  71+ A035                      ; coupled to ROM2/DOSEN. #BF selects the low ROM page with the
  72+ A035                      ; DOSEN-controlled low ROM select: BASIC-48 when DOSEN=0, TR-DOS
  73+ A035                      ; when DOSEN=1. This is the ATM2+ documented auto-TR-DOS mapping.
  74+ A035 3E BF                ld      a, #BF
  75+ A037 01 F7 3F             ld      bc, #3FF7
  76+ A03A ED 79                out     (c), a
  77+ A03C
  78+ A03C
  79+ A03C                      ; Map the alternate screen pages while the 32-byte mode trampoline
  80+ A03C                      ; is installed at #FFEC. Window 3 uses a fixed RAM page here; the
  81+ A03C                      ; TR-DOS auto-select mux is configured separately in window 0.
  82+ A03C 3E 78                ld      a, P126_VIDEO_PAGE7
  83+ A03E 01 F7 7F             ld      bc, #7FF7
  84+ A041 ED 79                out     (c), a
  85+ A043 3E 7C                ld      a, P126_VIDEO_PAGE3
  86+ A045 01 F7 FF             ld      bc, #FFF7
  87+ A048 ED 79                out     (c), a
  88+ A04A
  89+ A04A                      ; LD BC,#0177 / OUT (C),A / JP p126_atm_video_mode_ready
  90+ A04A 21 EC FF             ld      hl, #FFEC
  91+ A04D 36 01                ld      (hl), #01
  92+ A04F 23                   inc     hl
  93+ A050 36 77                ld      (hl), #77
  94+ A052 23                   inc     hl
  95+ A053 36 01                ld      (hl), #01
  96+ A055 23                   inc     hl
  97+ A056 36 ED                ld      (hl), #ED
  98+ A058 23                   inc     hl
  99+ A059 36 79                ld      (hl), #79
 100+ A05B 23                   inc     hl
 101+ A05C 36 C3                ld      (hl), #C3
 102+ A05E 23                   inc     hl
 103+ A05F 36 6E                ld      (hl), p126_atm_video_mode_ready & #FF
 104+ A061 23                   inc     hl
 105+ A062 36 A0                ld      (hl), p126_atm_video_mode_ready >> 8
 106+ A064
 107+ A064                      ; ATM Turbo 2+ RG=6: hardware text 80x25.
 108+ A064 3E 06                ld      a,P126_ATM_VIDEO_MODE_RG6_TEXT80X25
 109+ A066 32 00 A0             ld      (p126_atm_video_mode),a
 110+ A069 3E C6                ld      a, #C6
 111+ A06B C3 EC FF             jp      #FFEC
 112+ A06E
 113+ A06E              p126_atm_video_mode_ready:
 114+ A06E                      ; Restore CPU-visible host/staging pages. Keep D7 clear on #FFF7 so
 115+ A06E                      ; window 3 remains fixed to RAM page 0 for the TR-DOS staging buffer.
 116+ A06E 3E 7A                ld      a, P126_HOST_PAGE5
 117+ A070 01 F7 7F             ld      bc, #7FF7
 118+ A073 ED 79                out     (c), a
 119+ A075 3E 7F                ld      a, P126_HOST_PAGE0
 120+ A077 01 F7 FF             ld      bc, #FFF7
 121+ A07A ED 79                out     (c), a
 122+ A07C
 123+ A07C E1                   pop     hl
 124+ A07D D1                   pop     de
 125+ A07E C1                   pop     bc
 126+ A07F F1                   pop     af
 127+ A080 C9                   ret
 128+ A081
 129+ A081
 130+ A081              ; Reopen the permanent ATM shadow-port/DOSEN state after DS12887 access.
 131+ A081              ; #EFF7 bit 7 is the RTC gate; closing it also closes the permanent DOSEN
 132+ A081              ; path. Re-enter the TR-DOS #0177 service and restore RG6 text mode exactly
 133+ A081              ; as in Project126's atm_host implementation.
 134+ A081              p126_atm_restore_dosen:
 134+ A081
 135+ A081 F5                   push    af
 136+ A082 C5                   push    bc
 137+ A083 01 98 A0             ld      bc,p126_atm_restore_dosen_done
 138+ A086 C5                   push    bc
 139+ A087 01 53 2A             ld      bc,#2A53
 140+ A08A C5                   push    bc
 141+ A08B 01 77 01             ld      bc,#0177
 142+ A08E 3E 06                ld      a,P126_ATM_VIDEO_MODE_RG6_TEXT80X25
 143+ A090 32 00 A0             ld      (p126_atm_video_mode),a
 144+ A093 3E C6                ld      a,#C6
 145+ A095 C3 2F 3D             jp      #3D2F
 146+ A098              p126_atm_restore_dosen_done:
 147+ A098 C1                   pop     bc
 148+ A099 F1                   pop     af
 149+ A09A C9                   ret

 261+ A163              ; IN: HL=zero-terminated text, A=absolute row 0..24, C=column 0..79.
 262+ A163              ; Every static UI character is mirrored to port #EF.
 263+ A163              BIOS_DebugConsole_PrintAt:
 263+ A163
 264+ A163 F5                   push    af
 265+ A164 C5                   push    bc
 266+ A165 D5                   push    de
 267+ A166 E5                   push    hl
 268+ A167 CD E6 6D             call    p127_w3_ui_enter
 269+ A16A DA D3 A1             jp      c,.p126_print_at_denied
 270+ A16D                      ; The lease routine uses A; restore the complete caller frame before
 271+ A16D                      ; consuming the PrintAt row/column parameters.
 272+ A16D E1                   pop     hl
 273+ A16E D1                   pop     de
 274+ A16F C1                   pop     bc
 275+ A170 F1                   pop     af
 276+ A171 F5                   push    af
 277+ A172 C5                   push    bc
 278+ A173 D5                   push    de
 279+ A174 E5                   push    hl
 280+ A175 22 0D A0             ld      (bios_dbg_text_ptr), hl
 281+ A178 32 0F A0             ld      (bios_dbg_print_row), a
 282+ A17B 79                   ld      a, c
 283+ A17C 32 10 A0             ld      (bios_dbg_print_col), a
 284+ A17F              .p126_print_at_loop:
 285+ A17F 2A 0D A0             ld      hl, (bios_dbg_text_ptr)
 286+ A182 7E                   ld      a, (hl)
 287+ A183 B7                   or      a
 288+ A184 CA C0 A1             jp      z, .p126_print_at_done
 289+ A187 32 06 A0             ld      (bios_dbg_char), a
 290+ A18A 23                   inc     hl
 291+ A18B 22 0D A0             ld      (bios_dbg_text_ptr), hl
 292+ A18E 3A 0F A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_print_row)
 293+ A191 57                   ld      d, a
 294+ A192 3A 10 A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_print_col)
 295+ A195 5F                   ld      e, a
 296+ A196 3A 06 A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_char)
 297+ A199 F5                   push    af
 298+ A19A 01 EF 00             ld      bc,P127_UI_DEBUG_PORT
 299+ A19D ED 79                out     (c),a
 300+ A19F F1                   pop     af
 301+ A1A0 CD 77 A2             call    p126_screen_write_char
 302+ A1A3 3A 10 A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_print_col)
 303+ A1A6 3C                   inc     a
 304+ A1A7 FE 50                cp      BIOS_DEBUG_COLUMNS
 305+ A1A9 DA BA A1             jp      c, .p126_print_at_col_ok
 306+ A1AC AF                   xor     a
 307+ A1AD 32 10 A0             ld      (bios_dbg_print_col), a
 308+ A1B0 3A 0F A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_print_row)
 309+ A1B3 3C                   inc     a
 310+ A1B4 32 0F A0             ld      (bios_dbg_print_row), a
 311+ A1B7 C3 7F A1             jp      .p126_print_at_loop
 312+ A1BA              .p126_print_at_col_ok:
 313+ A1BA 32 10 A0             ld      (bios_dbg_print_col), a
 314+ A1BD C3 7F A1             jp      .p126_print_at_loop
 315+ A1C0              .p126_print_at_done:
 316+ A1C0 CD 01 6E             call    p127_w3_ui_leave
 317+ A1C3 01 EF 00             ld      bc,P127_UI_DEBUG_PORT
 318+ A1C6 3E 0D                ld      a,13
 319+ A1C8 ED 79                out     (c),a
 320+ A1CA 3E 0A                ld      a,10
 321+ A1CC ED 79                out     (c),a
 322+ A1CE E1                   pop     hl
 323+ A1CF D1                   pop     de
 324+ A1D0 C1                   pop     bc
 325+ A1D1 F1                   pop     af
 326+ A1D2 C9                   ret
 327+ A1D3              .p126_print_at_denied:
 328+ A1D3 E1                   pop     hl
 329+ A1D4 D1                   pop     de
 330+ A1D5 C1                   pop     bc
 331+ A1D6 F1                   pop     af
 332+ A1D7 C9                   ret
 333+ A1D8
 334+ A1D8              ; IN: A=character. CR/LF/BS are handled; printable ASCII is rendered.
 335+ A1D8              BIOS_DebugConsole_PrintChar:
 335+ A1D8

# file opened: p127_physmem.asm
   1+ A40C              ; ============================================================================
   2+ A40C              ; Project127 physical ATM RAM page test — Stage 2.23
   3+ A40C              ; Module version: 0.4.3
   4+ A40C              ; Runs from W2 (#A000..), with stack in W2. W1 resident/kernel remains fixed.
   5+ A40C              ; W3 is exclusively leased from the UI and restored to physical page 0.
   6+ A40C              ; This is the first general-purpose physical W3 remap test.
   7+ A40C              ; ============================================================================
   8+ A40C              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
   9+ A40C              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_MINOR EQU 4
  10+ A40C              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_PATCH EQU 3
  11+ A40C              P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_SIZE     EQU 04000h
  12+ A40C              P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE   EQU 0C000h
  13+ A40C              P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_PORT     EQU 0FFF7h
  14+ A40C
  15+ A40C              ; OUT: carry=1 on any failure. Starts with two pages, then allocates and
  16+ A40C              ; scans every remaining free physical page under one W3 ownership lease.
  17+ A40C              ; The probe byte is complemented so it can never equal the saved P0 byte.
  18+ A40C              ; If the dispatcher is not actually switching, only that one P0 byte is
  19+ A40C              ; touched and it is restored before returning. Full-page writes start only
  20+ A40C              ; after the physical switch has been proven.
  21+ A40C              p127_physmem_batch_selftest:
  22+ A40C 3E 53                ld      a,'S'
  23+ A40E CD BA AA             call    p127_physmem_trace
  24+ A411 AF                   xor     a
  25+ A412 32 7D AB             ld      (p127_physmem_error),a
  26+ A415 32 7E AB             ld      (p127_physmem_alloc_count),a
  27+ A418 32 7F AB             ld      (p127_physmem_quiesced),a

  29+ A41E 32 81 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_mapped),a
  30+ A421 32 82 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_saved_valid),a
  31+ A424

  33+ A424 3A A0 82             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)

  66+ A46E
  67+ A46E                      ; Preserve one byte from P0 for a non-destructive port-open probe.
  68+ A46E 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
  69+ A471 32 86 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_saved_p0),a
  70+ A474 2F                   cpl

  83+ A491

 224+ A5F8                      ; Stage 2.19: exhaust the allocator into a private page-ID list.
 225+ A5F8                      ; The first two pages are already listed. Each new allocation is
 226+ A5F8                      ; checked against all prior IDs before it is appended.

 265+ A63E                      ; First sweep: fill every allocated physical page with a unique
 266+ A63E                      ; address-derived pattern whose seed includes its physical page ID.

 291+ A670 CD BA AA             call    p127_physmem_trace

 293+ A673                      ; Reverse sweep verifies every page after all other pages have
 294+ A673                      ; already been written. This catches aliases and lost page contents.
 295+ A673 3A 7E AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_alloc_count)
 296+ A676 5F                   ld      e,a
 297+ A677 16 00                ld      d,0
 298+ A679 21 8B AB             ld      hl,p127_physmem_page_list
 299+ A67C 19                   add     hl,de

 317+ A6A1                      ; Reinitialize the reverse counter on first entry.
 318+ A6A1 B7                   or      a

 329+ A6B2                      ; Verify the allocator still marks every listed page ALLOCATED.
 330+ A6B2 CD 01 A9             call    p127_physmem_check_allocated

 335+ A6BF

 354+ A6DD              p127_physmem_fail_duplicate:
 355+ A6DD 3E 07                ld      a,7
 356+ A6DF C3 EE A6             jp      p127_physmem_set_error

 371+ A6F6                      ; Always return W3 to P0 before releasing UI ownership.
 372+ A6F6 3A 81 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_mapped)

 397+ A727

 419+ A74E 18 E7                jr      p127_physmem_cleanup_free_loop
 420+ A750              p127_physmem_cleanup_done:
 421+ A750                      ; Reallocation must return the same lowest free page and release it.
 422+ A750 3A 7D AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_error)

 424+ A754 20 21                jr      nz,p127_physmem_cleanup_return
 425+ A756 CD 2B 73             call    p127_mem_alloc

 437+ A774 DA B9 A8             jp      c,p127_physmem_cleanup_error
 438+ A777              p127_physmem_cleanup_return:

 440+ A77A B7                   or      a
 441+ A77B 20 05                jr      nz,p127_physmem_cleanup_return_error
 442+ A77D                      ; The live page sweep ran with W3 exclusively leased, so progress was
 443+ A77D                      ; mirrored to #EF only. Replay the compact page list now that W3=P0
 444+ A77D                      ; and the UI owner has been restored.

 446+ A780 B7                   or      a
 447+ A781 C9                   ret
 448+ A782              p127_physmem_cleanup_return_error:
 449+ A782 37                   scf
 450+ A783 C9                   ret
 451+ A784
 452+ A784              ; Replay all successful physical-page selections on the visible ATM console.
 453+ A784              ; The 57 IDs are compacted into two wrapped lines rather than 114 long lines.
 454+ A784              p127_physmem_replay_page_log:
 455+ A784                      ; The W3 sweep and its cleanup may leave the video-bank selector in a
 456+ A784                      ; different state even though the CPU windows have been restored.
 457+ A784                      ; Reapply the proven Project126/ATM RG6 setup only after the W3 lease
 458+ A784                      ; is released and UI ownership is active. This preserves screen RAM.
 459+ A784 CD 11 A0             call    p126_atm_video_init
 460+ A787
 461+ A787                      ; Fixed-row probe: if this label is visible, the selected alternate
 462+ A787                      ; text bank (P7 characters / P3 attributes) and PrintAt path agree.
 463+ A787 21 2E A8             ld      hl,p127_physmem_screen_video_line

 481+ A7AE 32 8A AB             ld      (p127_physmem_scan_remaining),a
 482+ A7B1 20 ED                jr      nz,p127_physmem_replay_fill_loop
 483+ A7B3 3E 0D                ld      a,13

 486+ A7BA CD D8 A1             call    BIOS_DebugConsole_PrintChar
 487+ A7BD
 488+ A7BD 21 AD A8             ld      hl,p127_physmem_screen_verify_label

 491+ A7C6 5F                   ld      e,a
 492+ A7C7 16 00                ld      d,0

 508+ A7E8 CD D8 A1             call    BIOS_DebugConsole_PrintChar
 509+ A7EB 3E 0A                ld      a,10
 510+ A7ED CD D8 A1             call    BIOS_DebugConsole_PrintChar
 511+ A7F0
 512+ A7F0                      ; Put the decisive result in the fixed status panel as well as the
 513+ A7F0                      ; scrolling log. PrintAt addresses rows directly, so this does not
 514+ A7F0                      ; depend on the current scrolling cursor after the long page replay.
 515+ A7F0 21 4E A8             ld      hl,p127_physmem_screen_status_line0

 524+ A805
 525+ A805              ; HL=zero-terminated string. The console routine preserves HL.
 526+ A805              p127_physmem_screen_puts:

 535+ A810
 536+ A810              ; A=page ID. Emit two hexadecimal digits through the visible UI console.
 537+ A810              p127_physmem_screen_hex8:

 570+ A8B9              p127_physmem_cleanup_error:
 571+ A8B9 3E FF                ld      a,0FFh
 572+ A8BB 32 7D AB             ld      (p127_physmem_error),a
 573+ A8BE 37                   scf
 574+ A8BF C9                   ret
 575+ A8C0
 576+ A8C0              ; Check the seven fixed reservation states: P0/P1/P2/P3/P4/P5/P7.
 577+ A8C0              ; OUT carry=1 if any protected page is not RESERVED.
 578+ A8C0              p127_physmem_check_reserved:
 579+ A8C0 21 EC 73             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 580+ A8C3 06 08                ld      b,8
 581+ A8C5 0E 00                ld      c,0
 582+ A8C7              p127_physmem_reserved_loop:
 583+ A8C7 79                   ld      a,c
 584+ A8C8 FE 06                cp      6
 585+ A8CA 28 2E                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_skip
 586+ A8CC FE 07                cp      7
 587+ A8CE 28 1A                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 588+ A8D0 FE 00                cp      0
 589+ A8D2 28 16                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 590+ A8D4 FE 01                cp      1
 591+ A8D6 28 12                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 592+ A8D8 FE 02                cp      2
 593+ A8DA 28 0E                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 594+ A8DC FE 03                cp      3
 595+ A8DE 28 0A                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 596+ A8E0 FE 04                cp      4
 597+ A8E2 28 06                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 598+ A8E4 FE 05                cp      5
 599+ A8E6 28 02                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 600+ A8E8 18 10                jr      p127_physmem_reserved_skip
 601+ A8EA              p127_physmem_reserved_check:
 602+ A8EA C5                   push    bc
 603+ A8EB E5                   push    hl
 604+ A8EC 59                   ld      e,c
 605+ A8ED 16 00                ld      d,0
 606+ A8EF 21 EC 73             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 607+ A8F2 19                   add     hl,de
 608+ A8F3 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 609+ A8F4 FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
 610+ A8F6 E1                   pop     hl
 611+ A8F7 C1                   pop     bc
 612+ A8F8 20 05                jr      nz,p127_physmem_reserved_fail
 613+ A8FA              p127_physmem_reserved_skip:
 614+ A8FA 0C                   inc     c
 615+ A8FB 10 CA                djnz    p127_physmem_reserved_loop
 616+ A8FD B7                   or      a
 617+ A8FE C9                   ret
 618+ A8FF              p127_physmem_reserved_fail:
 619+ A8FF 37                   scf
 620+ A900 C9                   ret
 621+ A901
 622+ A901              ; Every page in the private list must remain ALLOCATED during the sweep.
 623+ A901              p127_physmem_check_allocated:
 624+ A901 3A 7E AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_alloc_count)
 625+ A904 47                   ld      b,a
 626+ A905 21 8B AB             ld      hl,p127_physmem_page_list
 627+ A908              p127_physmem_alloc_state_loop:
 628+ A908 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 629+ A909 5F                   ld      e,a
 630+ A90A 16 00                ld      d,0
 631+ A90C C5                   push    bc
 632+ A90D E5                   push    hl
 633+ A90E 21 EC 73             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 634+ A911 19                   add     hl,de
 635+ A912 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 636+ A913 FE 02                cp      P127_MEM_ALLOCATED
 637+ A915 E1                   pop     hl
 638+ A916 C1                   pop     bc
 639+ A917 20 05                jr      nz,p127_physmem_alloc_state_fail
 640+ A919 23                   inc     hl
 641+ A91A 10 EC                djnz    p127_physmem_alloc_state_loop
 642+ A91C B7                   or      a
 643+ A91D C9                   ret
 644+ A91E              p127_physmem_alloc_state_fail:
 645+ A91E 37                   scf
 646+ A91F C9                   ret
 647+ A920
 648+ A920              ; After release, every non-reserved page must be FREE; reserved pages
 649+ A920              ; are checked separately by p127_physmem_check_reserved.
 650+ A920              p127_physmem_check_free_state:
 651+ A920 21 EC 73             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 652+ A923 06 40                ld      b,64
 653+ A925 0E 00                ld      c,0
 654+ A927              p127_physmem_free_state_loop:
 655+ A927 79                   ld      a,c
 656+ A928 FE 00                cp      0
 657+ A92A 28 1E                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 658+ A92C FE 01                cp      1
 659+ A92E 28 1A                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 660+ A930 FE 02                cp      2
 661+ A932 28 16                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 662+ A934 FE 03                cp      3
 663+ A936 28 12                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 664+ A938 FE 04                cp      4
 665+ A93A 28 0E                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 666+ A93C FE 05                cp      5
 667+ A93E 28 0A                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 668+ A940 FE 07                cp      7
 669+ A942 28 06                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 670+ A944 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 671+ A945 B7                   or      a
 672+ A946 20 0D                jr      nz,p127_physmem_free_state_fail
 673+ A948 18 05                jr      p127_physmem_free_state_next
 674+ A94A              p127_physmem_free_state_reserved:
 675+ A94A 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 676+ A94B FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
 677+ A94D 20 06                jr      nz,p127_physmem_free_state_fail
 678+ A94F              p127_physmem_free_state_next:
 679+ A94F 23                   inc     hl
 680+ A950 0C                   inc     c
 681+ A951 10 D4                djnz    p127_physmem_free_state_loop
 682+ A953 B7                   or      a
 683+ A954 C9                   ret
 684+ A955              p127_physmem_free_state_fail:
 685+ A955 37                   scf
 686+ A956 C9                   ret
 687+ A957
 688+ A957              ; Emit one printable phase marker directly on the established #EF debug port.
 689+ A957              ; This remains available while the UI is quiesced and W3 is leased.
 690+ A957
 691+ A957              ; ============================================================================
 692+ A957              ; Stage 2.20 — guarded physical-page API.
 693+ A957              ; All code and stack remain in W2. The only hardware mapping operation is W3.
 694+ A957              ; Public ABI:
 695+ A957              ;   alloc_page: OUT A=allocated page, carry=1 if exhausted.
 696+ A957              ;   free_page:  IN A=allocated page, carry=1 if invalid or lease active.
 697+ A957              ;   w3_begin:  quiesce UI, acquire W3, save P0 byte; DI remains in force.
 698+ A957              ;   w3_map:    IN A=allocated page; valid only inside an active W3 lease.
 699+ A957              ;   w3_end:    restore W3=P0 and saved byte, release lease, resume UI.
 700+ A957              ; No API maps W0/W1/W2, frees a currently mapped page, or enables IRQs.
 701+ A957              ; ============================================================================
 702+ A957              p127_physmem_alloc_page:
 703+ A957 C3 2B 73             jp      p127_mem_alloc
 704+ A95A
 705+ A95A              p127_physmem_free_page:
 706+ A95A 32 B7 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_page_arg),a
 707+ A95D 3A B2 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 708+ A960 B7                   or      a
 709+ A961 C2 14 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 710+ A964 3A B7 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 711+ A967 47                   ld      b,a
 712+ A968 3A A0 82             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
 713+ A96B B8                   cp      b
 714+ A96C CA 14 AA             jp      z,p127_physmem_api_reject
 715+ A96F 3A B7 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 716+ A972 C3 41 73             jp      p127_mem_free
 717+ A975
 718+ A975              ; Begin a bounded W3 lease. Bootstrap mapping must be W3=P0.
 719+ A975              p127_physmem_w3_begin:
 720+ A975 F3                   di
 721+ A976 3A B2 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 722+ A979 B7                   or      a
 723+ A97A C2 14 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 724+ A97D 3A A0 82             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
 725+ A980 B7                   or      a
 726+ A981 C2 14 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 727+ A984 CD 06 6E             call    p127_w3_quiesce
 728+ A987 D8                   ret     c
 729+ A988 3E 01                ld      a,1
 730+ A98A 32 B3 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 731+ A98D CD 25 6E             call    p127_w3_handoff_acquire
 732+ A990 38 15                jr      c,p127_physmem_w3_begin_rollback
 733+ A992 3E 01                ld      a,1
 734+ A994 32 B4 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_acquired),a
 735+ A997 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
 736+ A99A 32 B6 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_p0),a
 737+ A99D 3E 01                ld      a,1
 738+ A99F 32 B5 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_valid),a
 739+ A9A2 32 B2 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_active),a
 740+ A9A5 B7                   or      a
 741+ A9A6 C9                   ret
 742+ A9A7              p127_physmem_w3_begin_rollback:
 743+ A9A7 CD 4D 6E             call    p127_w3_resume
 744+ A9AA AF                   xor     a
 745+ A9AB 32 B3 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 746+ A9AE 37                   scf
 747+ A9AF C9                   ret
 748+ A9B0
 749+ A9B0              ; Map only an allocator-owned page into W3.
 750+ A9B0              p127_physmem_w3_map:
 751+ A9B0 32 B7 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_page_arg),a
 752+ A9B3 3A B2 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 753+ A9B6 FE 01                cp      1
 754+ A9B8 C2 14 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 755+ A9BB 3A B7 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 756+ A9BE FE 40                cp      P127_PHYSMEM_MAX_PAGES
 757+ A9C0 D2 14 AA             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_reject
 758+ A9C3 5F                   ld      e,a
 759+ A9C4 16 00                ld      d,0
 760+ A9C6 21 EC 73             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 761+ A9C9 19                   add     hl,de
 762+ A9CA 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 763+ A9CB FE 02                cp      P127_MEM_ALLOCATED
 764+ A9CD C2 14 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 765+ A9D0 3A B7 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 766+ A9D3 C3 40 AB             jp      p127_physmem_set_w3
 767+ A9D6
 768+ A9D6              ; End the lease in recovery order. If release/resume fails, state flags are
 769+ A9D6              ; retained so the caller can retry this routine without losing ownership.
 770+ A9D6              p127_physmem_w3_end:
 771+ A9D6 3A B2 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 772+ A9D9 FE 01                cp      1
 773+ A9DB C2 14 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 774+ A9DE AF                   xor     a
 775+ A9DF CD 40 AB             call    p127_physmem_set_w3
 776+ A9E2 D8                   ret     c
 777+ A9E3 3A B5 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_saved_valid)
 778+ A9E6 B7                   or      a
 779+ A9E7 28 06                jr      z,p127_physmem_w3_end_release
 780+ A9E9 3A B6 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_saved_p0)
 781+ A9EC 32 00 C0             ld      (P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE),a
 782+ A9EF              p127_physmem_w3_end_release:
 783+ A9EF 3A B4 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_acquired)
 784+ A9F2 B7                   or      a
 785+ A9F3 28 08                jr      z,p127_physmem_w3_end_resume
 786+ A9F5 CD 39 6E             call    p127_w3_handoff_release
 787+ A9F8 D8                   ret     c
 788+ A9F9 AF                   xor     a
 789+ A9FA 32 B4 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_acquired),a
 790+ A9FD              p127_physmem_w3_end_resume:
 791+ A9FD 3A B3 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_quiesced)
 792+ AA00 B7                   or      a
 793+ AA01 28 08                jr      z,p127_physmem_w3_end_done
 794+ AA03 CD 4D 6E             call    p127_w3_resume
 795+ AA06 D8                   ret     c
 796+ AA07 AF                   xor     a
 797+ AA08 32 B3 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 798+ AA0B              p127_physmem_w3_end_done:
 799+ AA0B AF                   xor     a
 800+ AA0C 32 B5 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_valid),a
 801+ AA0F 32 B2 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_active),a
 802+ AA12 B7                   or      a
 803+ AA13 C9                   ret
 804+ AA14
 805+ AA14              p127_physmem_api_reject:
 806+ AA14 37                   scf
 807+ AA15 C9                   ret
 808+ AA16
 809+ AA16              ; Runtime API contract test. It proves rejection paths, allocated-page-only
 810+ AA16              ; mapping, mapped-page free protection, P0 byte restoration and UI recovery.
 811+ AA16              p127_physmem_api_selftest:
 812+ AA16 AF                   xor     a
 813+ AA17 32 B2 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_active),a
 814+ AA1A 32 B3 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 815+ AA1D 32 B4 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_acquired),a
 816+ AA20 32 B5 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_valid),a
 817+ AA23 3E 01                ld      a,1
 818+ AA25 CD 5A A9             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 819+ AA28 D2 B0 AA             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 820+ AA2B CD 57 A9             call    p127_physmem_alloc_page
 821+ AA2E DA B0 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 822+ AA31 32 B8 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_test_page),a
 823+ AA34 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
 824+ AA37 32 B9 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_test_p0),a
 825+ AA3A CD 75 A9             call    p127_physmem_w3_begin
 826+ AA3D DA AA AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_free
 827+ AA40                      ; A reserved page must never be accepted by the map API.
 828+ AA40 3E 01                ld      a,1
 829+ AA42 CD B0 A9             call    p127_physmem_w3_map
 830+ AA45 D2 A7 AA             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_test_end
 831+ AA48 3A B8 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 832+ AA4B CD B0 A9             call    p127_physmem_w3_map
 833+ AA4E DA A7 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_end
 834+ AA51                      ; The currently mapped page cannot be freed while the lease is held.
 835+ AA51 3A B8 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 836+ AA54 CD 5A A9             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 837+ AA57 D2 A7 AA             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_test_end
 838+ AA5A 3E A5                ld      a,0A5h
 839+ AA5C 32 00 C0             ld      (P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE),a
 840+ AA5F CD D6 A9             call    p127_physmem_w3_end
 841+ AA62 DA B0 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 842+ AA65 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
 843+ AA68 47                   ld      b,a
 844+ AA69 3A B9 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_p0)
 845+ AA6C B8                   cp      b
 846+ AA6D C2 B0 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 847+ AA70 3A B8 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 848+ AA73 CD 5A A9             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 849+ AA76 DA B0 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 850+ AA79                      ; First-fit must return the same page after release.
 851+ AA79 CD 57 A9             call    p127_physmem_alloc_page
 852+ AA7C DA B0 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 853+ AA7F 47                   ld      b,a
 854+ AA80 3A B8 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 855+ AA83 B8                   cp      b
 856+ AA84 C2 B0 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 857+ AA87 78                   ld      a,b
 858+ AA88 CD 5A A9             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 859+ AA8B DA B0 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 860+ AA8E 3A 73 6F             ld      a,(p127_w3_state)
 861+ AA91 FE 01                cp      P127_W3_STATE_UI_ACTIVE
 862+ AA93 C2 B0 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 863+ AA96 3A 74 6F             ld      a,(p127_w3_owner)
 864+ AA99 FE 01                cp      P127_W3_OWNER_UI
 865+ AA9B C2 B0 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 866+ AA9E 3A A0 82             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
 867+ AAA1 B7                   or      a
 868+ AAA2 C2 B0 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 869+ AAA5 B7                   or      a
 870+ AAA6 C9                   ret
 871+ AAA7
 872+ AAA7              p127_physmem_api_test_end:
 873+ AAA7 CD D6 A9             call    p127_physmem_w3_end
 874+ AAAA              p127_physmem_api_test_free:
 875+ AAAA 3A B8 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 876+ AAAD CD 5A A9             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 877+ AAB0              p127_physmem_api_test_fail:
 878+ AAB0 37                   scf
 879+ AAB1 C9                   ret

 890+ AABA              p127_physmem_trace:
 891+ AABA 01 EF 00             ld      bc,00EFh
 892+ AABD ED 79                out     (c),a

 894+ AAC0
 895+ AAC0              ; Emit progress for each physical page while the UI is quiesced.
 896+ AAC0              ; A='F' prints FILL PAGE ID=xx; A='V' prints VERIFY PAGE ID=xx.
 897+ AAC0              ; Preserve every register because the caller owns the page-list cursor.
 898+ AAC0              p127_physmem_trace_page:
 899+ AAC0 F5                   push    af
 900+ AAC1 C5                   push    bc
 901+ AAC2 D5                   push    de
 902+ AAC3 E5                   push    hl
 903+ AAC4 FE 46                cp      'F'
 904+ AAC6 20 05                jr      nz,p127_physmem_trace_page_verify
 905+ AAC8 21 12 AB             ld      hl,p127_physmem_trace_fill_text
 906+ AACB 18 03                jr      p127_physmem_trace_page_text
 907+ AACD              p127_physmem_trace_page_verify:
 908+ AACD 21 20 AB             ld      hl,p127_physmem_trace_verify_text
 909+ AAD0              p127_physmem_trace_page_text:
 910+ AAD0 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 911+ AAD1 B7                   or      a
 912+ AAD2 28 08                jr      z,p127_physmem_trace_page_value
 913+ AAD4 23                   inc     hl
 914+ AAD5 E5                   push    hl
 915+ AAD6 CD 0C AB             call    p127_physmem_trace_char
 916+ AAD9 E1                   pop     hl
 917+ AADA 18 F4                jr      p127_physmem_trace_page_text
 918+ AADC              p127_physmem_trace_page_value:
 919+ AADC 3A 89 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_current_page)
 920+ AADF CD F1 AA             call    p127_physmem_trace_hex8
 921+ AAE2 3E 0D                ld      a,13
 922+ AAE4 CD 0C AB             call    p127_physmem_trace_char
 923+ AAE7 3E 0A                ld      a,10
 924+ AAE9 CD 0C AB             call    p127_physmem_trace_char
 925+ AAEC E1                   pop     hl
 926+ AAED D1                   pop     de
 927+ AAEE C1                   pop     bc
 928+ AAEF F1                   pop     af
 929+ AAF0 C9                   ret
 930+ AAF1
 931+ AAF1              p127_physmem_trace_hex8:
 932+ AAF1 F5                   push    af
 933+ AAF2 E6 F0                and     0F0h
 934+ AAF4 0F                   rrca
 935+ AAF5 0F                   rrca

 975+ AB54
 976+ AB54              ; IN D=pattern seed. Byte(address)=ROL(address_low) XOR address_high XOR D.
 977+ AB54              ; The same address produces a different expected value at each seed.
 978+ AB54              p127_physmem_fill_pattern:
 979+ AB54 21 00 C0             ld      hl,P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE
 980+ AB57 01 00 40             ld      bc,P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_SIZE
 981+ AB5A              .p127_physmem_fill_pattern_loop:

 984+ AB5C AC                   xor     h
 985+ AB5D AA                   xor     d
 986+ AB5E 77                   ld      (hl),a

 988+ AB60 0B                   dec     bc
 989+ AB61 78                   ld      a,b
 990+ AB62 B1                   or      c
 991+ AB63 20 F5                jr      nz,.p127_physmem_fill_pattern_loop
 992+ AB65 C9                   ret
 993+ AB66
 994+ AB66              ; IN D=pattern seed. Verify all 16 KiB against the address-derived pattern.
 995+ AB66              p127_physmem_verify_pattern:

1002+ AB6F AA                   xor     d
1003+ AB70 BE                   cp      (hl)
